    lot_id,
    lot_number,
    status,
    current_quantity,
    unit,
    style_code,
    shipment_code,
    facility_name,
    facility_city,

    country_code,
    depth,
    path_ids,
    path_string
) AS (
    SELECT
        l.id,
        l.lot_number,
        CAST(l.status AS VARCHAR(50)),
        l.current_quantity,
        CAST(l.unit AS VARCHAR(50)),
        l.style_code,
        l.shipment_code,
        f.name,
        f.location_city,
        p.name,
        CAST(p.party_type AS VARCHAR(50)),
        p.country_code,
        0,
        '/' || CAST(l.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/',
        CAST(l.lot_number AS VARCHAR(2000))
    FROM lots l
    JOIN facilities f ON l.current_facility_id = f.id
    JOIN parties p ON f.party_id = p.id
    WHERE l.id = ?

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        parent_lot.id,
        parent_lot.lot_number,
        CAST(parent_lot.status AS VARCHAR(50)),
        parent_lot.current_quantity,
        CAST(parent_lot.unit AS VARCHAR(50)),
        parent_lot.style_code,
        parent_lot.shipment_code,
        f.name,
        f.location_city,
        p.name,
        CAST(p.party_type AS VARCHAR(50)),
        p.country_code,
        bt.depth + 1,
        bt.path_ids || CAST(parent_lot.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/',
        CAST(bt.path_string || ' <- ' || parent_lot.lot_number AS VARCHAR(2000))
    FROM backward_trace bt
    JOIN lot_lineage_edges e ON bt.lot_id = e.child_lot_id
    JOIN lots parent_lot ON e.parent_lot_id = parent_lot.id
    JOIN facilities f ON parent_lot.current_facility_id = f.id
    JOIN parties p ON f.party_id = p.id
    WHERE bt.depth < 50
    AND bt.path_ids NOT LIKE '%/' || CAST(parent_lot.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/%'
)
SELECT
    lot_id,
    lot_number,
    status,
    current_quantity,
    unit,
    style_code,
    shipment_code,
    facility_name,
    facility_city,
    party_name,
    party_type,
    country_code,
    depth,
    path_string
FROM backward_trace
ORDER BY depth ASC
]"}

{"timestamp":"2026-11-21T00:30:46.201+0600","level":"DEBUG","traceId":"none","spanId":"none","logger":"o.s.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate","message":"Executing prepared SQL query"}
{"timestamp":"2026-11-21T00:30:46.201+0600","level":"DEBUG","traceId":"none","spanId":"none","logger":"o.s.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate","message":"Executing prepared SQL statement [WITH RECURSIVE backward_trace(
    lot_id,
    lot_number,
    status,
    current_quantity,
    unit,
    style_code,
    shipment_code,
    facility_name,
    facility_city,
    party_name,
    party_type,
    country_code,
    depth,
    path_ids,
    path_string
) AS (
    SELECT
        l.id,
        l.lot_number,
        CAST(l.status AS VARCHAR(50)),
        l.current_quantity,
        CAST(l.unit AS VARCHAR(50)),
        l.style_code,
        l.shipment_code,
        f.name,
        f.location_city,
        p.name,
        CAST(p.party_type AS VARCHAR(50)),
        p.country_code,
        0,
        '/' || CAST(l.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/',
        CAST(l.lot_number AS VARCHAR(2000))
    FROM lots l
    JOIN facilities f ON l.current_facility_id = f.id
    JOIN parties p ON f.party_id = p.id
    WHERE l.id = ?

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        parent_lot.id,
        parent_lot.lot_number,
        CAST(parent_lot.status AS VARCHAR(50)),
        parent_lot.current_quantity,
        CAST(parent_lot.unit AS VARCHAR(50)),
        parent_lot.style_code,
        parent_lot.shipment_code,
        f.name,
        f.location_city,
        p.name,
        CAST(p.party_type AS VARCHAR(50)),
        p.country_code,
        bt.depth + 1,
        bt.path_ids || CAST(parent_lot.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/',
        CAST(bt.path_string || ' <- ' || parent_lot.lot_number AS VARCHAR(2000))
    FROM backward_trace bt
    JOIN lot_lineage_edges e ON bt.lot_id = e.child_lot_id
    JOIN lots parent_lot ON e.parent_lot_id = parent_lot.id
    JOIN facilities f ON parent_lot.current_facility_id = f.id
    JOIN parties p ON f.party_id = p.id
    WHERE bt.depth < 50
    AND bt.path_ids NOT LIKE '%/' || CAST(parent_lot.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/%'
)
SELECT
    lot_id,
    lot_number,
    status,
    current_quantity,
    unit,
    style_code,
    shipment_code,
    facility_name,
    facility_city,
    party_name,
    party_type,
    country_code,
    depth,
    path_string
FROM backward_trace
ORDER BY depth ASC
]"}

    JOIN facilities f ON l.current_facility_id = f.id
    JOIN parties p ON f.party_id = p.id
    WHERE l.id = ?

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        parent_lot.id,
        parent_lot.lot_number,
        CAST(parent_lot.status AS VARCHAR(50)),
        parent_lot.current_quantity,
        CAST(parent_lot.unit AS VARCHAR(50)),
        parent_lot.style_code,
        parent_lot.shipment_code,
        f.name,
        f.location_city,
        p.name,
        CAST(p.party_type AS VARCHAR(50)),
        p.country_code,
        bt.depth + 1,
        bt.path_ids || CAST(parent_lot.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/',
        CAST(bt.path_string || ' <- ' || parent_lot.lot_number AS VARCHAR(2000))
    FROM backward_trace bt
    JOIN lot_lineage_edges e ON bt.lot_id = e.child_lot_id
    JOIN lots parent_lot ON e.parent_lot_id = parent_lot.id
    JOIN facilities f ON parent_lot.current_facility_id = f.id
    JOIN parties p ON f.party_id = p.id
    WHERE bt.depth < 50
    AND bt.path_ids NOT LIKE '%/' || CAST(parent_lot.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/%'
)
SELECT
    lot_id,
    lot_number,
    status,
    current_quantity,
    unit,
    style_code,
    shipment_code,
    facility_name,
    facility_city,
    party_name,
    party_type,
    country_code,
    depth,
    path_string
FROM backward_trace
ORDER BY depth ASC
]"}

    lot_number,
    status,
    current_quantity,

        CAST(l.unit AS VARCHAR(50)),
        l.style_code,

        p.name,

        CAST(l.lot_number AS VARCHAR(2000))
    FROM lots l
    JOIN facilities f ON l.current_facility_id = f.id
    JOIN parties p ON f.party_id = p.id
    WHERE l.id = ?

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        parent_lot.id,
        parent_lot.lot_number,
        CAST(parent_lot.status AS VARCHAR(50)),
        parent_lot.current_quantity,
        CAST(parent_lot.unit AS VARCHAR(50)),

        CAST(p.party_type AS VARCHAR(50)),
        p.country_code,
        bt.depth + 1,
        bt.path_ids || CAST(parent_lot.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/',
        CAST(bt.path_string || ' <- ' || parent_lot.lot_number AS VARCHAR(2000))
    FROM backward_trace bt
    JOIN lot_lineage_edges e ON bt.lot_id = e.child_lot_id
    JOIN lots parent_lot ON e.parent_lot_id = parent_lot.id
    JOIN facilities f ON parent_lot.current_facility_id = f.id
    JOIN parties p ON f.party_id = p.id
    WHERE bt.depth < 50
    AND bt.path_ids NOT LIKE '%/' || CAST(parent_lot.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/%'
)

    status,
    current_quantity,
    unit,

    shipment_code,

ORDER BY depth ASC

    status,
    current_quantity,
    unit,

    facility_city,

        l.current_quantity,
        CAST(l.unit AS VARCHAR(50)),
        l.style_code,
        l.shipment_code,

        f.location_city,
        p.name,
        CAST(p.party_type AS VARCHAR(50)),
        p.country_code,
        0,
        '/' || CAST(l.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/',
        CAST(l.lot_number AS VARCHAR(2000))
    FROM lots l
    JOIN facilities f ON l.current_facility_id = f.id
    JOIN parties p ON f.party_id = p.id
    WHERE l.id = ?

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        parent_lot.id,
        parent_lot.lot_number,
        CAST(parent_lot.status AS VARCHAR(50)),
        parent_lot.current_quantity,
        CAST(parent_lot.unit AS VARCHAR(50)),
        parent_lot.style_code,
        parent_lot.shipment_code,

        CAST(p.party_type AS VARCHAR(50)),
        p.country_code,
        bt.depth + 1,
        bt.path_ids || CAST(parent_lot.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/',
        CAST(bt.path_string || ' <- ' || parent_lot.lot_number AS VARCHAR(2000))
    FROM backward_trace bt
    JOIN lot_lineage_edges e ON bt.lot_id = e.child_lot_id
    JOIN lots parent_lot ON e.parent_lot_id = parent_lot.id
    JOIN facilities f ON parent_lot.current_facility_id = f.id
    JOIN parties p ON f.party_id = p.id
    WHERE bt.depth < 50
    AND bt.path_ids NOT LIKE '%/' || CAST(parent_lot.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/%'
)
SELECT
    lot_id,
    lot_number,
    status,
    current_quantity,
    unit,
    style_code,
    shipment_code,
    facility_name,
    facility_city,
    party_name,
    party_type,
    country_code,
    depth,
    path_string
FROM backward_trace
ORDER BY depth ASC
]"}

    status,
    current_quantity,
    unit,

        CAST(p.party_type AS VARCHAR(50)),
        p.country_code,
        0,
        '/' || CAST(l.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/',
        CAST(l.lot_number AS VARCHAR(2000))
    FROM lots l
    JOIN facilities f ON l.current_facility_id = f.id
    JOIN parties p ON f.party_id = p.id
    WHERE l.id = ?

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        parent_lot.id,
        parent_lot.lot_number,
        CAST(parent_lot.status AS VARCHAR(50)),
        parent_lot.current_quantity,
        CAST(parent_lot.unit AS VARCHAR(50)),
        parent_lot.style_code,
        parent_lot.shipment_code,

        f.location_city,

        CAST(p.party_type AS VARCHAR(50)),
        p.country_code,
        bt.depth + 1,
        bt.path_ids || CAST(parent_lot.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/',
        CAST(bt.path_string || ' <- ' || parent_lot.lot_number AS VARCHAR(2000))
    FROM backward_trace bt
    JOIN lot_lineage_edges e ON bt.lot_id = e.child_lot_id
    JOIN lots parent_lot ON e.parent_lot_id = parent_lot.id
    JOIN facilities f ON parent_lot.current_facility_id = f.id
    JOIN parties p ON f.party_id = p.id
    WHERE bt.depth < 50
    AND bt.path_ids NOT LIKE '%/' || CAST(parent_lot.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/%'
)
SELECT
    lot_id,
    lot_number,
    status,
    current_quantity,
    unit,
    style_code,
    shipment_code,
    facility_name,
    facility_city,
    party_name,
    party_type,
    country_code,
    depth,
    path_string
FROM backward_trace
ORDER BY depth ASC
]"}

        parent_lot.id,
        parent_lot.lot_number,

        parent_lot.current_quantity,

    FROM backward_trace bt

    JOIN lots parent_lot ON e.parent_lot_id = parent_lot.id
    JOIN facilities f ON parent_lot.current_facility_id = f.id

ORDER BY depth ASC
]"}

{"timestamp":"2026-11-21T00:30:48.283+0600","level":"DEBUG","traceId":"none","spanId":"none","logger":"o.s.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate","message":"Executing prepared SQL statement [WITH RECURSIVE backward_trace(
    lot_id,
    lot_number,
    status,
    current_quantity,
    unit,
    style_code,
    shipment_code,
    facility_name,
    facility_city,
    party_name,
    party_type,
    country_code,
    depth,
    path_ids,
    path_string
) AS (
    SELECT
        l.id,
        l.lot_number,
        CAST(l.status AS VARCHAR(50)),
        l.current_quantity,
        CAST(l.unit AS VARCHAR(50)),
        l.style_code,
        l.shipment_code,
        f.name,
        f.location_city,
        p.name,
        CAST(p.party_type AS VARCHAR(50)),
        p.country_code,
        0,
        '/' || CAST(l.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/',
        CAST(l.lot_number AS VARCHAR(2000))
    FROM lots l
    JOIN facilities f ON l.current_facility_id = f.id
    JOIN parties p ON f.party_id = p.id
    WHERE l.id = ?

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        parent_lot.id,
        parent_lot.lot_number,
        CAST(parent_lot.status AS VARCHAR(50)),
        parent_lot.current_quantity,
        CAST(parent_lot.unit AS VARCHAR(50)),
        parent_lot.style_code,
        parent_lot.shipment_code,
        f.name,
        f.location_city,
        p.name,
        CAST(p.party_type AS VARCHAR(50)),
        p.country_code,
        bt.depth + 1,
        bt.path_ids || CAST(parent_lot.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/',
        CAST(bt.path_string || ' <- ' || parent_lot.lot_number AS VARCHAR(2000))
    FROM backward_trace bt
    JOIN lot_lineage_edges e ON bt.lot_id = e.child_lot_id
    JOIN lots parent_lot ON e.parent_lot_id = parent_lot.id
    JOIN facilities f ON parent_lot.current_facility_id = f.id
    JOIN parties p ON f.party_id = p.id
    WHERE bt.depth < 50
    AND bt.path_ids NOT LIKE '%/' || CAST(parent_lot.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/%'
)
SELECT
    lot_id,
    lot_number,
    status,
    current_quantity,
    unit,
    style_code,
    shipment_code,
    facility_name,
    facility_city,
    party_name,
    party_type,
    country_code,
    depth,
    path_string
FROM backward_trace
ORDER BY depth ASC
]"}

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        parent_lot.id,
        parent_lot.lot_number,
        CAST(parent_lot.status AS VARCHAR(50)),
        parent_lot.current_quantity,
        CAST(parent_lot.unit AS VARCHAR(50)),

        CAST(bt.path_string || ' <- ' || parent_lot.lot_number AS VARCHAR(2000))
    FROM backward_trace bt
    JOIN lot_lineage_edges e ON bt.lot_id = e.child_lot_id
    JOIN lots parent_lot ON e.parent_lot_id = parent_lot.id
    JOIN facilities f ON parent_lot.current_facility_id = f.id
    JOIN parties p ON f.party_id = p.id
    WHERE bt.depth < 50
    AND bt.path_ids NOT LIKE '%/' || CAST(parent_lot.id AS VARCHAR(100)) || '/%'
)
SELECT

    unit,

    party_type,
    country_code,
    depth,
    path_string
FROM backward_trace
ORDER BY depth ASC
]"}
